# Commands · L · part 3

1,439 entries, legal-deadline to linkedin-post. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **legal-deadline**: Gestiona plazos legales de casos con alertas automáticas. Subcomandos: set (crear plazo), list (listar con filtros), alert (mostrar próximos), complete (marcar completado), extend (extender plazo con justificación). ([gonzalezpazmonica/savia](https://github.com/gonzalezpazmonica/savia/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/legal-deadline.md) · ★49)
- **legal-decision-memo**: Document a legal decision in the project's decision log — the question, alternatives with pros, cons and legal risk, the chosen option and rationale, accepted risk, approvers and revisit triggers. Use when the user has made a legal call and wants a record of why and what risk they accepted. Not for weighing a team or management decision; see /leadership-decision. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-decision-memo.md) · ★4)
- **legal-dispute**: Work a dispute: chronology, claims matrix, procedure and deadlines, assessment, strategy, submissions (legal-dispute) ([mobeetle/legalsuite](https://github.com/mobeetle/legalsuite/blob/HEAD/commands/legal-dispute.md) · ★1)
- **legal-doc**: Create a professional legal document for enterprise use. ([YousefNabil-SOC/claude-apex](https://github.com/YousefNabil-SOC/claude-apex/blob/HEAD/commands/legal-doc.md) · ★4)
- **legal-doc-check**: Prueft ein einzelnes Dokument (AGB / Datenschutzerklaerung / Impressum / Widerrufsbelehrung / Cookie-Richtlinie) auf rechtliche Maengel nach DE/EU-Recht — ohne Codebase-Kontext. Output: Finding-Liste + Clean-Version. ([FutureRootsDE/legal-audit-de](https://github.com/FutureRootsDE/legal-audit-de/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/legal-doc-check.md) · ★38)
- **legal-docs**: Draft lightweight legal copy — terms of service, privacy policy, disclaimers, cookie notices, and data processing agreements. Use for early-stage products that need functional legal text before engaging a lawyer. ([SongD-85/pm-ops](https://github.com/SongD-85/pm-ops/blob/HEAD/pm-toolkit/commands/legal-docs.md))
- **legal-draft**: Draft contracts, demand letters, discovery responses, motions, and legal memoranda ([alexclowe/awesome-claude-cowork-plugins](https://github.com/alexclowe/awesome-claude-cowork-plugins/blob/HEAD/paralegal/commands/legal-draft.md) · ★31)
- **legal-drafting**: Draft or revise legal text first (precedent, reuse vocabulary, cover note); review runs in the same door (legal-drafting) ([mobeetle/legalsuite](https://github.com/mobeetle/legalsuite/blob/HEAD/commands/legal-drafting.md) · ★1)
- **legal-employment-agreement**: Draft an employment contract or independent contractor agreement — duties, pay, IP assignment, restrictive covenants, confidentiality and termination, flagging misclassification risk and state or country rules. Use when the user is hiring an employee or engaging a contractor and needs the agreement written. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-employment-agreement.md) · ★4)
- **legal-gdpr-compliance**: Analyze GDPR obligations legally — data mapping, lawful basis, DPIA and DPO needs, data subject rights procedures, breach notification, transfer mechanisms and Article 30 records. Use when the user asks how to legitimize EU data transfers, whether they need a DPIA or DPO, or what GDPR legally requires of them. Not for a scored control-gap assessment; see /compliance-gdpr. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-gdpr-compliance.md) · ★4)
- **legal-goal**: Define a checkable legal success condition for /legal-loop. Accepts a named profile (citations-clean, draft-passes-gate, adversarial-converge, nda-batch-clean, reg-watch, timeline-sourced) or free-text objective. Produces a persisted Goal Record — never starts work itself. ([fedec65/bettercallclaude](https://github.com/fedec65/bettercallclaude/blob/HEAD/bettercallclaude/commands/legal-goal.md) · ★47)
- **legal-hr**: Prepare HR/legal review packets and review sensitive employee-action language ([clous-ai/plugins](https://github.com/clous-ai/plugins/blob/HEAD/clous-hr/commands/legal-hr.md))
- **legal-incident-response-plan**: Create a legal data breach response plan — severity levels, response team, containment, investigation, notification deadlines by jurisdiction, communication templates and tabletop exercises. Use when the user has no written plan for a data breach and needs one drafted, through regulator and customer notices. Not for technical playbooks; see /security-incident-response. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-incident-response-plan.md) · ★4)
- **legal-intake**: Qualify a lead, engage fast, capture the story and open the matter (legal-intake) ([mobeetle/legalsuite](https://github.com/mobeetle/legalsuite/blob/HEAD/commands/legal-intake.md) · ★1)
- **legal-ip-review**: Review the intellectual property terms in an agreement — background and foreground IP, assignment and work-for-hire, license grants, open-source conflicts, freedom to operate and common IP traps. Use when the user asks who will own the work, code or inventions under a contract, or whether a license grant goes too far. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-ip-review.md) · ★4)
- **legal-jurisdiction-analysis**: Analyze legal requirements across every country or state a business touches — registration, data protection, employment and consumer law — with a comparison matrix, conflicts and a recommended baseline. Use when the user operates, hires or stores data in several countries or states and asks how their obligations differ or conflict. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-jurisdiction-analysis.md) · ★4)
- **legal-kb**: Laedt gezielt einen Knowledge-Base-Chunk (Gesetz/Thema/Urteil/Checkliste/Behoerde/Anwalts-Empfehlung) in den aktuellen Kontext. ([FutureRootsDE/legal-audit-de](https://github.com/FutureRootsDE/legal-audit-de/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/legal-kb.md) · ★38)
- **legal-legal-audit**: Audit a business's existing legal documents — contracts, policies, terms and governance records — against standards, finding missing, expired and non-compliant items ranked by severity with a remediation order. Use when the user wants to know what legal paperwork they are missing or which documents are out of date. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-legal-audit.md) · ★4)
- **legal-legal-glossary**: Generate a plain-language glossary of legal terms by area — contracts, privacy, IP, employment, compliance and corporate — with an example and why each term matters to non-lawyers. Use when the user or their team keeps hitting legal jargon and wants the terms explained in everyday words. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-legal-glossary.md) · ★4)
- **legal-legal-playbook**: Create a legal playbook for recurring scenarios — contract negotiation, breach response, terminations, IP disputes, vendor onboarding — with decision trees, approval thresholds, escalation rules and turnaround times per team. Use when the user wants sales, HR, engineering or procurement to handle routine legal situations without asking legal every time. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-legal-playbook.md) · ★4)
- **legal-legal-research**: Research one legal question in statutes, regulations, case law and commentary, with a confidence level, open ambiguities and whether to see an attorney. Use when the user asks what the law says on a concrete point, like whether they need a state license to operate. Not for a quoted clause or a whole-law briefing; see /legal-clause-analysis or /compliance-regulation-research. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-legal-research.md) · ★4)
- **legal-loop**: Iterate a worker-evaluator cycle against a Goal Record until the success condition is met or a stop limit is reached. The evaluator (a different agent than the worker) judges each iteration using MCP verification tools. Produces an auditable verdict trail and a final MET / NOT MET status. ([fedec65/bettercallclaude](https://github.com/fedec65/bettercallclaude/blob/HEAD/bettercallclaude/commands/legal-loop.md) · ★47)
- **legal-nda**: Generate a mutual or one-way Non-Disclosure Agreement — definition of confidential information, standard exclusions, permitted use, term and survival, return of materials, remedies and governing law. Use when the user is about to share confidential information with another party and needs an NDA drafted. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-nda.md) · ★4)
- **legal-negotiation-prep**: Prepare a negotiation strategy for a contract — ideal, compromise and walk-away positions per contentious clause, counter-arguments, redlines, trade-offs and BATNA. Use when the user is about to negotiate a specific contract with a counterparty and wants to know what to push for and what to concede. ([bromso/singularskills](https://github.com/bromso/singularskills/blob/HEAD/adapters/claude/commands/legal-negotiation-prep.md) · ★4)
- **legal-open-source-policy**: Create an open-source usage and contribution policy — approved, restricted and prohibited licenses, dependency approval, license scanning in CI, contribution and CLA rules, a compatibility matrix and SBOM requirements. - **lfe-compete**: You are acting as a senior framework architect conducting a structured competitive analysis against Library-First Engineering (LFE). - **lfe-complexity-check**: Inspector sub-skill. Analyses changed code for cyclomatic complexity, excessive nesting, oversized functions, and cognitive load indicators. Pure LLM reasoning — no external tooling. Writes .plans/checks/complexity_findings.md. Called by lfe-inspector when enabled in inspector-config.md. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-complexity-check.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-dep-audit**: Inspector sub-skill. Reviews dependency manifest files (package.json, requirements.txt, go.mod, Cargo.toml, pom.xml) changed in the current diff for risky version patterns and stale majors. Emits a human-run audit instruction rather than executing tools. Writes .plans/checks/dep_findings.md. Called by lfe-inspector when enabled in inspector-config.md. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-dep-audit.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-diagnose**: Disciplined bug-diagnosis loop. Reproduce → Hypothesise → Fix. Use in the Inspector sub-pipeline (Step 3, conditional) when verification fails. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-diagnose.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-extract-domain**: Conduct a "Day 0" interview with the founder to populate the project's domain-knowledge.md and CONTEXT.md. Use when the agent encounters an undocumented Black Box. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-extract-domain.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-grill-with-docs**: Deep alignment interview that challenges intent against the domain model, sharpens terminology, and writes back to CONTEXT.md and ADRs inline. Use at the start of any Major Change in the Architect sub-pipeline. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-grill-with-docs.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-hygiene**: Conduct a structural audit of the repository to detect "Documentation Drift" or "Spaghetti Files." Use in Phase 5 (Hygiene sub-pipeline, Step 1). ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-hygiene.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-improve-architecture**: Find deepening opportunities in the codebase — turn shallow modules into deep ones. Use in Phase 5 (Hygiene sub-pipeline) or when scheduled by session count. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-improve-architecture.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-inspector**: Act as the Inspector for an LFE-compliant project. Use when verifying an implementation against domain truth, validation baselines, or debugging regressions. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-inspector.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-mutation-verify**: Inspector sub-skill. Prompt-based mutation testing — reasons about whether existing tests would catch hypothetical code mutations. No test runner required. Writes .plans/checks/mutation_findings.md. Called by lfe-inspector when enabled in inspector-config.md. Enable only for critical or safety-sensitive modules. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-mutation-verify.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-perf-check**: Inspector sub-skill. Analyses the implementation diff for common performance anti-patterns — resource leaks, N+1 queries, unbounded growth, algorithmic complexity. Pure LLM reasoning, no profilers. Writes .plans/checks/perf_findings.md. Called by lfe-inspector when enabled in inspector-config.md. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-perf-check.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-plan-critique**: Run a 5-lens pre-build critique of the approved active plan before the Builder starts. Acts as the Architect persona, read-only on src/. Writes .plans/plan_critique.md. Use immediately after Brain approves active_plan.md. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-plan-critique.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-scout**: Rapid maintenance for minor fixes (max 3 files). Bypasses the full pipeline for non-architectural content edits. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-scout.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-security-check**: Inspector sub-skill. Runs an OWASP Top-10 prompt-based security analysis on the current implementation diff. No external tooling required — pure LLM reasoning. Writes .plans/checks/security_findings.md. Called by lfe-inspector when enabled in inspector-config.md. ([StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering](https://github.com/StChiotis/Library-First-Engineering/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/lfe-security-check.md) · ★44)
- **lfe-session**: You are acting as a senior framework architect helping improve the Library-First Engineering (LFE) framework. This is a META session — you are working ON the framework, not WITH it. Do NOT run the LFE pipeline (`/lfe-boot`, `/lfe-architect`, etc.) — those are for product repos that use LFE as a template. - **line.cs**: Jak nakreslit úsečku v AutoCADu příkazem ÚSEČKA (LINE, zkratka U), výzva po výzvě: body určené nebo zadané, Zpět, Uzavři a navázání. Vyzkoušejte online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.cs.md))
- **line.de**: So arbeitet AutoCADs LINIE (LINE, Alias L): Punkte wählen oder tippen, Zurück und Schließen, und die Eingabetaste, um von der letzten Linie weiterzuzeichnen. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.de.md))
- **line.es**: Cómo funciona LINEA (LINE, alias L) de AutoCAD en español, solicitud a solicitud: puntos señalados o escritos, desHacer y Cerrar. Pruébalo gratis online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.es.md))
- **line.fr**: Comment fonctionne LIGNE (LINE, alias L) d'AutoCAD en français, invite par invite : points cliqués ou tapés, annuler, clore, Entrée pour continuer. Essayez-le. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.fr.md))
- **line.hu**: Az AutoCAD VONAL (LINE, VO) parancsa promptról promptra: kattintott vagy beírt pontok, Vissza és Bezárás, Enter az utolsó vonal folytatásához. Próbálja ki. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.hu.md))
- **line.it**: Come funziona LINEA (LINE, alias L) di AutoCAD in italiano, richiesta per richiesta: punti selezionati o digitati, Annulla e Chiudi, Invio per proseguire. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.it.md))
- **line.ja**: AutoCAD の LINE[線分](L)の使い方をプロンプトごとに解説します。点の指定と座標入力、元に戻す、閉じる、[Enter]で最後の線分から続けて作図。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.ja.md))
- **line.ko**: AutoCAD 선(LINE, L)을 프롬프트 순서대로 설명합니다: 클릭하거나 입력한 점, 명령 취소와 닫기, Enter 키로 마지막 선에서 이어 그리기. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.ko.md))
- **line.pl**: Jak narysować linię w AutoCADzie poleceniem LINIA (LINE, skrót L), monit po monicie: punkty wskazane lub wpisane, Cofaj, Zamknij i kontynuacja. Wypróbuj online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.pl.md))
- **line.pt-BR**: Como funciona o LINE do AutoCAD em português, LINHA (alias L), prompt a prompt: pontos indicados ou digitados, Desfazer e Fechar. Experimente grátis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/line.pt-BR.md))
